# Third-party licenses

The open-source license and copyright notices for every package in this build of the HAX web component distribution. Every package is at `es6/node_modules/<package>` in the build. The package index gives each package's license and which license text below applies to it. A license text that is identical across packages is printed once and shared by number. Per-package package.json and LICENSE files are intentionally not shipped with the build; this file is the attribution record. Each package remains under its own license as noted.

```text
The layout of this theme (site menu on the left, page in the middle, the
headings of the page on the right) pays homage to the Hugo Book theme
(https://github.com/alex-shpak/hugo-book), MIT License, Copyright (c) 2018
Alex Shpak.

This package contains no code, styles, templates, fonts, icons or other
assets from Hugo Book. It is written for HAXcms from DDD design tokens and
HAXcms theme parts. It is an independent work and is not affiliated with or
endorsed by the author of Hugo Book.

The themes in lib/ are built on book-theme and pay homage to the themes
below on the same terms: no code, styles, templates, fonts, icons or other
assets from any of them, independent works, not affiliated with or endorsed
by their authors. Three are named apart from the theme they honor because
that name belongs to a company or project brand.

  just-the-docs-theme  Just the Docs (Jekyll), MIT License
                       https://github.com/just-the-docs/just-the-docs
                       Copyright (c) 2016 Patrick Marsceill

  handbook-theme       Docsy (Hugo), Apache License 2.0
                       https://github.com/google/docsy
                       Copyright 2018 Google LLC

  manual-theme         the classic theme of Docusaurus, MIT License
                       https://github.com/facebook/docusaurus
                       Copyright (c) Facebook, Inc. and its affiliates

  guidebook-theme      Starlight (Astro), MIT License
                       https://github.com/withastro/starlight
                       Copyright (c) 2023 Astro contributors

Docsy, Docusaurus and Starlight are names of their respective owners and are
used here only to say which layouts these themes honor.
```
